def test_chronicle_instantiates(tmp_path):

    c = Chronicle(cfg=cfg, client=MagicMock())
    assert c is not None

def test_chronicle_add_and_retrieve(tmp_path):
    """Chronicle add() and retrieve() work correctly under new name."""
    from unittest.mock import MagicMock
    from memory.chronicle import Chronicle
    cfg = MagicMock()
    cfg.storage.vectors_path = str(tmp_path / 'test_chronicle.pkl')
    cfg.models.embeddings = "text-embedding-3-small"

```python
def test_chronicle_add_and_retrieve(tmp_path):
    """Chronicle add() and query() work correctly under new name."""
    from unittest.mock import MagicMock
    from memory.chronicle import Chronicle
    cfg = MagicMock()
    cfg.storage.vectors_path = str(tmp_path / 'test_chronicle.pkl')
    cfg.models.embeddings = "text-embedding-3-small"
    cfg.memory.min_relevance_score = 0.0
    mock_client = MagicMock()
    mock_client.embeddings.create.return_value.data[0].embedding = [0.1] * 1536
    c = Chronicle(cfg=cfg, client=mock_client)
    c.add('The ArcaOgnitorium uses cosine similarity for retrieval')

    assert isinstance(results[0], dict)

Claude:  Your `compose()` method is here:

```python
def compose(self) -> ComposeResult:
    with Horizontal():
        self.left = LeftMenuPane(id="left")
        self.middle = ActiveChatPane(id="middle")
        self.right = HistoryPane(id="right")
        yield self.left
        yield self.middle
        yield self.right
```

Add the StatusLayer import at the top of the file with the other UI imports, 
then mount it inside `compose()` like this:

```python
from ui.widgets.status_layer import StatusLayer
```

```python
def compose(self) -> ComposeResult:
    with Vertical():
        with Horizontal():
            self.left = LeftMenuPane(id="left")
            self.middle = ActiveChatPane(id="middle")
            self.right = HistoryPane(id="right")
            yield self.left
            yield self.middle
            yield self.right
        self.status_layer = StatusLayer(id="status_layer")
        yield self.status_layer
```

The outer `Horizontal` becomes wrapped in a `Vertical` so the StatusLayer sits 
below the three panes. You'll also need a CSS rule to control its height — 
add this to the `CSS` block:

```css
#status_layer { height: 3; border: round white; }
```

Boot the app and confirm it renders before wiring any real data into it.

```python
from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass

@dataclass

@dataclass
class BubbleMessage:
"""All data needed to render a single conversation bubble."""
speaker_id: str # 'wizard' | 'luminarious' | entity_id
display_name: str # 'WIZARD' | 'LUMINARIOUS' | 'THE CONTRARIAN'
content: str # Full message text (may be partial during streaming)
model_id: str # Model that handled this turn
timestamp: str # ISO format
color_hex: str # Speaker color — Wizard uses pale, others use entity color
is_entity: bool = False # True if an Entity interruption

5.2 ui/rendering/bubbles.py
◆ Populates existing stub. Provides three bubble widget classes and a 
BubbleFactory helper. Called from app.py message rendering path.

from __future__ import annotations
from textual.widget import Widget
from textual.app import ComposeResult
from textual.containers import Horizontal
from rich.text import Text
from rich.panel import Panel
from rich.style import Style
from dataclasses import dataclass


class WizardBubble(Widget):
"""Right-aligned bubble for Wizard input.
Parchment background (#D4C8A8 tint). No header bar. Heavier visual weight.
Width: 70% of pane. Aligned: right.
"""
def __init__(self, message: BubbleMessage) -> None:
super().__init__()
self.message = message

def render(self) -> Panel:
"""
Render as Rich Panel:
- border_style: dim gold (#B8860B at 40% opacity via dim)
- title: None (Wizard bubbles have no header)
- content: message.content in parchment (#D4C8A8)
- subtitle: timestamp in mist (#5A6070)
- padding: (0, 1)
"""
...


class LuminariousBubble(Widget):
"""Left-aligned bubble for Luminarious and all Entity responses.
Header bar in speaker color. Background tint at 10% opacity of speaker color.
Supports streaming — content updates reactively as chunks arrive.
"""
def __init__(self, message: BubbleMessage) -> None:
super().__init__()
self.message = message
self._content = message.content

def append_chunk(self, chunk: str) -> None:
"""Append streaming chunk to content. Triggers reactive re-render."""
self._content += chunk
self.refresh()

def render(self) -> Panel:
"""
Render as Rich Panel:
- title: '[bold]SPEAKER_NAME[/] ◆ model_id ◆ timestamp' in speaker color
- border_style: speaker color hex
- content: self._content in pale (#D4C8A8)
- If uninvited Entity: append ' ↯' to title
- If chronicle_hit: append ' ✦' to subtitle
- padding: (0, 1)
- Entity bubbles: width 60% of pane (vs 80% for Luminarious)
"""
...


class BubbleFactory:
"""Creates the appropriate bubble widget for a given BubbleMessage."""

@staticmethod
def create(message: BubbleMessage) -> Widget:
"""
Return WizardBubble if message.speaker_id == 'wizard'.
Return LuminariousBubble for all other speakers.
The LuminariousBubble handles both Luminarious and Entity responses —
the speaker color in BubbleMessage determines the visual identity.
"""
if message.speaker_id == 'wizard':
return WizardBubble(message)
return LuminariousBubble(message)

**`WizardBubble.render()`:**
```python
def render(self) -> Panel:
    content = Text(self.message.content, style="color(#D4C8A8)")
    return Panel(
        content,
        border_style="dim #B8860B",
        subtitle=Text(self.message.timestamp, style="#5A6070"),
        padding=(0, 1),
    )
```

**`LuminariousBubble.render()`:**
```python
def render(self) -> Panel:
    title = Text()
    title.append(self.message.display_name, style=f"bold 
#{self.message.color_hex}")
    title.append(f" ◆ {self.message.model_id} ◆ {self.message.timestamp}", 
style=f"#{self.message.color_hex}")
    if self.message.uninvited:
        title.append(" ↯", style=f"#{self.message.color_hex}")

    subtitle = None
    if self.message.chronicle_hit:
        subtitle = Text("✦", style=f"#{self.message.color_hex}")

    content = Text(self._content, style="#D4C8A8")
    return Panel(
        content,
        title=title,
        subtitle=subtitle,
        border_style=f"#{self.message.color_hex}",
        padding=(0, 1),
    )
```

Claude:  The stub is well-specified. The `_check_perf` method is already fully 
implemented. You need to fill in the four `...` worker bodies. Replace each 
`...` with the pseudocode translated to real code:

**`_glyph_drift_worker`:**
```python
def _glyph_drift_worker(self) -> None:
    worker = get_current_worker()
    while not worker.is_cancelled:
        t0 = time.monotonic()
        glyph = random.choice(self.config.glyph_pool)
        position = random.randint(0, 63)
        self.app.call_from_thread(self.app.update_glyph_field, position, glyph)
        elapsed_ms = (time.monotonic() - t0) * 1000
        if not self._check_perf('glyph_drift', elapsed_ms):
            return
        time.sleep(GLYPH_INTERVAL)
```

**`_color_temp_worker`:**
```python
def _color_temp_worker(self) -> None:
    worker = get_current_worker()
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    while not worker.is_cancelled:
        t0 = time.monotonic()
        elapsed = t0 - t_start
        r_val = 13 + int(8 * math.sin(2 * math.pi * elapsed / TEMP_INTERVAL))
        new_bg = f'#{r_val:02X}0B0E'
        self.app.call_from_thread(self.app.set_css_variable, '--app-bg', new_bg)
        elapsed_ms = (time.monotonic() - t0) * 1000
        if not self._check_perf('color_temp', elapsed_ms):
            return
        time.sleep(0.1)
```

**`_bg_pulse_worker`:**
```python
def _bg_pulse_worker(self) -> None:
    worker = get_current_worker()
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    while not worker.is_cancelled:
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
        pulse = 0.5 + 0.5 * math.sin(2 * math.pi * elapsed / PULSE_INTERVAL)
        opacity = 0.06 + 0.04 * pulse
        self.app.call_from_thread(self.app.set_sigil_opacity, opacity)
        time.sleep(0.5)
```

**`_event_worker`:**
```python
def _event_worker(self, event_type: str, entity_color: str | None) -> None:
    if event_type == 'entity_interrupt' and entity_color:
        css_class = f'entity-pulse-{entity_color}'
        self.app.call_from_thread(self.app.query_one('#chat-pane').add_class, 
css_class)
        time.sleep(0.8)
        
self.app.call_from_thread(self.app.query_one('#chat-pane').remove_class, 
css_class)

    elif event_type == 'distillation':
        self.app.call_from_thread(self.app.query_one('#right').add_class, 
'distillation-ripple')
        time.sleep(1.2)
        self.app.call_from_thread(self.app.query_one('#right').remove_class, 
'distillation-ripple')

    elif event_type == 'chronicle_retrieve':
        self.app.call_from_thread(self.app.status_layer.update_status, 
chronicle_retrieved=True)
        time.sleep(0.8)
        self.app.call_from_thread(self.app.status_layer.update_status, 
chronicle_retrieved=False)

    elif event_type in ('grimoire_inject', 'tome_inject', 
'reflection_generated'):
        self.app.call_from_thread(self.app.status_layer.add_class, event_type)
        time.sleep(0.6)
        self.app.call_from_thread(self.app.status_layer.remove_class, 
event_type)
```

LordFingers: async def _run_boot_sequence(self) -> None:
        """
        Boot sequence procedure:
        1. Render dark screen with single sigil from sigils/ directory
           - Load random .txt file from sigils/ directory
           - If sigils/ empty, use fallback: single '◆' centered
           - Display at low opacity in center of chat pane
        2. Render ASCII title via pyfiglet
           - Select random font from config.boot.banner_fonts list
           - Call: pyfiglet.figlet_format('LUMINARIOUS', font=font)
           - Display in title banner region in gold color
        3. Display opening line
           - Select random line from config.boot.boot_lines list
           - Render in center pane in mist italic
           - Await asyncio.sleep(1.5)  # Let Wizard read it
        4. Resolve interface
           - Remove boot overlay
           - Mount StatusLayer, left menu, history pane (already composed)
           - StatusLayer initial state populated from config
        5. Focus Invocation Field
        """
        ...

Claude:  Here's the implementation. Add it to `app.py` as a new method on `ChatTUIApp`, 
and call `await self._run_boot_sequence()` at the end of `on_mount()`:

```python
async def _run_boot_sequence(self) -> None:
    if not self.cfg.raw.get('boot', {}).get('boot_enabled', True):
        return

    from pathlib import Path
    import random
    import pyfiglet
    from textual.widgets import Static

    # 1. Sigil
    sigil_text = '◆'
    sigil_files = list(Path('sigils').glob('*.txt'))
    if sigil_files:
        sigil_text = random.choice(sigil_files).read_text()

    sigil = Static(sigil_text, classes='boot_sigil', markup=False)
    await self.middle.current_turn.mount(sigil)
    await asyncio.sleep(0.8)

    # 2. ASCII title banner
    banner_fonts = self.cfg.raw.get('boot', {}).get('banner_fonts', ['slant'])
    font = random.choice(banner_fonts)
    try:
        banner_text = pyfiglet.figlet_format('LUMINARIOUS', font=font)
    except Exception:
        banner_text = 'LUMINARIOUS'

    banner = Static(f'[bold #C9A84C]{banner_text}[/]', classes='boot_banner')
    await self.middle.current_turn.mount(banner)
    await asyncio.sleep(0.6)

    # 3. Opening line
    boot_lines = self.cfg.raw.get('boot', {}).get('boot_lines', ['The fire is 
lit.'])
    line = random.choice(boot_lines)
    opening = Static(f'[italic #5A6070]{line}[/]', classes='boot_line')
    await self.middle.current_turn.mount(opening)
    await asyncio.sleep(1.5)

    # 4. Remove boot overlay
    sigil.remove()
    banner.remove()
    opening.remove()

    # 5. Focus
    self.middle.focus_input()
```

Then add to `config.yaml`:

```yaml
boot:
  boot_enabled: true
  banner_fonts:
    - slant
    - big
    - banner3
  boot_lines:
    - "The fire is lit. What troubles the Wizard?"
    - "The Arca Cognitorium stirs."
    - "All threads accounted for. The Chronicle is ready."
    - "Luminarious attends."
```

And add CSS for the boot elements:

.boot_banner { color: #C9A84C; height: auto; }
.boot_line { color: #5A6070; height: auto; }
```

LordFingers: """
    The Conjuration Chamber — all configuration controls.

        ('The Awakening', 'boot'),

    ]

        """
        Render each section as a collapsible Vertical block.
        Each config key gets an appropriate widget:
          bool → Switch
          str (short) → Input
          str (enum) → Select
          int → Input with type='integer'
          list → comma-separated Input or multi-line TextArea
        Section headers styled in copper, keys in parchment.
        """
        yield Label('◆  CONJURATION CHAMBER  ◆', id='conjure-title')
        with ScrollableContainer():
            for section_name, config_key in self.SECTIONS:
                yield self._build_section(section_name, config_key)
    def _build_section(self, name: str, key: str) -> Vertical:
        """Build a config section block from config.yaml[key] dict."""
        ...
    def on_switch_changed(self, event: Switch.Changed) -> None:
        """Write bool change to config.yaml immediately."""
        ...
    def on_input_submitted(self, event: Input.Submitted) -> None:
        """Write string/int change to config.yaml on submit."""
        ...
    def _write_config(self, section: str, key: str, value) -> None:
        """
        Atomic config write:
        1. Load current config.yaml
        2. Update config[section][key] = value
        3. Write to config.yaml.tmp
        4. Rename tmp → config.yaml
        5. Post ConfigChanged message to app for real-time updates
        """
        ...from __future__ import annotations
from textual.app import ComposeResult
from textual.screen import Screen
from textual.widgets import Label, Switch, Input, Select, Button, Static
from textual.containers import ScrollableContainer, Horizontal, Vertical
import yaml
from pathlib import Path
class ConjurationPage(Screen):
    """

Claude:  Here's the full implementation:

```python
from __future__ import annotations
import yaml
from pathlib import Path
from textual.app import ComposeResult, App
from textual.screen import Screen
from textual.widgets import Label, Switch, Input, Select, Button, Static
from textual.containers import ScrollableContainer, Horizontal, Vertical
from textual.message import Message


CONFIG_PATH = Path('config.yaml')


class ConjurationPage(Screen):
    """
    The Conjuration Chamber — all configuration controls.
    Sections mirror config.yaml structure with lore names.
    Changes write to config.yaml immediately on widget change event.
    Non-restart settings apply in real-time via app message passing.
    """

    class ConfigChanged(Message):
        """Posted to app when config.yaml is updated."""
        def __init__(self, section: str, key: str, value) -> None:
            super().__init__()
            self.section = section
            self.key = key
            self.value = value

    SECTIONS = [
        ('The Aether',      'api'),
        ('The Vessels',     'memory'),
        ('The Oracle',      'routing'),
        ('The Awakening',   'boot'),
        ('The Vestments',   'conjuration'),
        ('The Emanations',  'animations'),
        ('The Sanctum',     'layout'),
        ('The Conclave',    'entities'),
    ]

    def compose(self) -> ComposeResult:
        yield Label('◆  CONJURATION CHAMBER  ◆', id='conjure-title')
        with ScrollableContainer():
            for section_name, config_key in self.SECTIONS:
                yield self._build_section(section_name, config_key)
        yield Button('◀  BACK', id='conjure-back')

    def _build_section(self, name: str, key: str) -> Vertical:
        cfg = self._load_config()
        section_data = cfg.get(key, {})

        block = Vertical(classes='conjure-section')
        block.mount(Label(f'◆ {name.upper()}', 
classes='conjure-section-header'))

        if not isinstance(section_data, dict):
            block.mount(Static(f'({key}: no editable keys)', 
classes='conjure-empty'))
            return block

        for k, v in section_data.items():
            row = Horizontal(classes='conjure-row')
            row.mount(Label(k, classes='conjure-key'))

            widget_id = f'cfg__{key}__{k}'

            if isinstance(v, bool):
                widget = Switch(value=v, id=widget_id)
            elif isinstance(v, int):
                widget = Input(value=str(v), id=widget_id, type='integer')
            elif isinstance(v, list):
                widget = Input(value=', '.join(str(i) for i in v), id=widget_id)
            else:
                widget = Input(value=str(v) if v is not None else '', 
id=widget_id)

            row.mount(widget)
            block.mount(row)

        return block

    def on_switch_changed(self, event: Switch.Changed) -> None:
        widget_id = event.switch.id or ''
        if not widget_id.startswith('cfg__'):
            return
        _, section, key = widget_id.split('__', 2)
        self._write_config(section, key, event.value)

    def on_input_submitted(self, event: Input.Submitted) -> None:
        widget_id = event.input.id or ''
        if not widget_id.startswith('cfg__'):
            return
        _, section, key = widget_id.split('__', 2)

        # Coerce type to match original config value
        cfg = self._load_config()
        original = cfg.get(section, {}).get(key)
        value = event.value

        if isinstance(original, bool):
            value = value.lower() in ('true', '1', 'yes')
        elif isinstance(original, int):
            try:
                value = int(value)
            except ValueError:
                return
        elif isinstance(original, list):
            value = [v.strip() for v in value.split(',')]

        self._write_config(section, key, value)

    def on_button_pressed(self, event: Button.Pressed) -> None:
        if event.button.id == 'conjure-back':
            self.app.pop_screen()

    def _write_config(self, section: str, key: str, value) -> None:
        try:
            cfg = self._load_config()
            if section not in cfg:
                cfg[section] = {}
            cfg[section][key] = value

            tmp = CONFIG_PATH.with_suffix('.yaml.tmp')
            tmp.write_text(yaml.dump(cfg, default_flow_style=False, 
allow_unicode=True))
            tmp.rename(CONFIG_PATH)

            self.post_message(self.ConfigChanged(section, key, value))
        except Exception as e:
            self.app.log.error(f'Conjuration write failed: {e}')

    def _load_config(self) -> dict:
        try:
            return yaml.safe_load(CONFIG_PATH.read_text()) or {}
        except Exception:
            return {}
```

And add CSS:

```css
#conjure-title { color: #C9A84C; text-style: bold; padding: 1 2; }
.conjure-section { height: auto; margin: 1 0; }
.conjure-section-header { color: #B87333; text-style: bold; padding: 0 1; }
.conjure-row { height: auto; padding: 0 1; }
.conjure-key { color: #D4C8A8; width: 30; }
.conjure-empty { color: #5A6070; padding: 0 2; }
```

The spec describes `ConversationsPage` as a simple list view — it's a 
`Screen` overlay, not a replacement for the left pane. It's a separate 
full-screen browser that gets pushed onto the screen stack and loads a 
conversation when selected.

The import path is wrong in the spec stub (`storage.conversation_store` doesn't 
exist — it's `memory.conversation_store`). Here's the implementation:

```python
from __future__ import annotations
from textual.app import ComposeResult
from textual.screen import Screen
from textual.widgets import ListView, ListItem, Label, Static
from textual.containers import Vertical
from memory.conversation_store import ConversationStore


class ConversationSelected(Exception):
    pass


class ConversationsPage(Screen):
    """Conversation browser. Lists all past Threads with metadata."""

    def __init__(self, store: ConversationStore) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.store = store

    def compose(self) -> ComposeResult:
        yield Label('◆  CONVERSATIONS  ◆', id='convpage-title')
        convos = self.store.list()
        if not convos:
            yield Static('(no conversations yet)', classes='convpage-empty')
            return
        with ListView(id='convpage-list'):
            for c in convos:
                title = c.get('title') or '(untitled)'
                updated = c.get('updated_at', '')[:16] or '—'
                cid = c.get('id', '')
                yield ListItem(
                    Label(f'{title}  [{updated}]'),
                    id=f'conv__{cid}'
                )

    def on_list_view_selected(self, event: ListView.Selected) -> None:
        item_id = event.item.id or ''
        if not item_id.startswith('conv__'):
            return
        cid = item_id[6:]
        self.dismiss(cid)
```

The key design decision: `self.dismiss(cid)` passes the selected conversation 
ID back to whoever pushed this screen. In `app.py`, when you push this screen 
you provide a callback:

```python
self.push_screen(ConversationsPage(self.conversations), 
self._on_conversation_selected)

def _on_conversation_selected(self, cid: str | None) -> None:
    if cid:
        self.conversations.load(cid)
        self._render_full_history_from_store(clear_first=True)
        self._render_legend()
```

And CSS:

```css
#convpage-title { color: #C9A84C; text-style: bold; padding: 1 2; }
.convpage-empty { color: #5A6070; padding: 1 2; }
```

Log this as a deviation — the spec import path `storage.conversation_store` 
corrected to `memory.conversation_store`.

Claude:  **Decision Log**
- `StatusState` moved to `ui/state.py` to avoid circular import with 
`status_layer.py`
- `BubbleMessage` added to `ui/state.py` alongside `StatusState` — pure data, 
no UI dependencies
- Animation workers stubbed as no-ops (`update_glyph_field`, 
`set_css_variable`, `set_sigil_opacity`) — visual wiring deferred
- `glyph_pool` using placeholder symbols — Wizard-authored glyphs to replace 
later, no structural change needed
- `ConversationsPage` uses `dismiss(cid)` pattern to pass selection back to 
caller via callback
- `ConversationSelected` exception class removed — unused
- `AnimationConfig.master_enabled` defaults to `False` — animations dormant 
until individually confirmed
- App location remains `ui/app.py` not `client/app.py` — moving would break 
imports

breaks imports. Permanent. |

`ui/state.py` before anything could boot

expected (`config.py`, `reflection.py`, config keys)

test failure; resolved by installing to pyenv directly

`_run_boot_sequence` — required cleanup
- Animation workers crashed immediately on `master_enabled=True` because 
app-side receiver methods didn't exist — stubbed to unblock

LordFingers: def _build_context(self, user_text: str, thread_id: str) -> List[Dict]:
        conv = self.conversations.active
        if conv is None:
            return [{"role": "user", "content": user_text}]
        mem_cfg = self.cfg.memory
        messages: List[Dict] = []
        thread = self.conversations.get_thread(thread_id)
        if thread.summary:
            messages.append({"role": "system", "content": f"Conversation 
summary:\n{thread.summary}"})
        # Phase 3 decision:
        # retrieval is project-scoped on conversation_ids BUT thread-restricted 
per conversation

proj_id else [conv.id]

        thread_by_conv: Dict[str, str] = {}
        for cid in allowed_ids:
            if cid == conv.id:
                thread_by_conv[cid] = conv.active_thread_id
            else:
                thread_by_conv[cid] = 
self.conversations.peek_active_thread_id(cid)
        retrieved = self.vectors.query(
            user_text,
            top_k=int(mem_cfg.retrieve_top_k),
            conversation_ids=allowed_ids,
            thread_by_conversation=thread_by_conv,
        )
        if retrieved:
            blob = "\n\n".join(f"[score={r['score']:.3f}] {r['text']}" for r in 
retrieved)
            messages.append({"role": "system", "content": "Relevant long-term 
memory:\n" + blob})
        short_max = int(mem_cfg.short_term_max_messages)
        for m in thread.messages[-short_max:]:
            messages.append({"role": m.get("role", ""), "content": 
m.get("content", "")})
        if not messages or messages[-1]["role"] != "user" or 
messages[-1]["content"] != user_text:
            messages.append({"role": "user", "content": user_text})

LordFingers: async def _handle_grimoire_command(self, args: list[str]) -> None:
"""
Dispatch /grimoire subcommands.
args: list of tokens after /grimoire

Dispatch table:
[] → push GrimoirePage screen
["add", ...] → parse content/category, call grimoire.add()
["list"] → render active entries as system bubble
["remove", entry_id] → call grimoire.remove(), confirm
["restore", entry_id] → call grimoire.restore(), confirm
["edit", entry_id, *content] → call grimoire.edit(), confirm
["status"] → render token_usage() as system bubble
unknown → render help text as system bubble

For add with inline category (format: "cat: content"):
if ":" in args[1]:
category, content = args[1].split(":", 1)
else:
category = "general"
content = " ".join(args[1:])
"""
...

Claude:  Here's the implementation:

```python
async def _handle_grimoire_command(self, args: list[str]) -> None:
    if not args:
        # push GrimoirePage — placeholder until GrimoirePage is built
        self._set_status("Grimoire page not yet implemented. Use /grimoire 
list.")
        return

    sub = args[0].lower()

    if sub == "add":
        if len(args) < 2:
            self._set_status("Usage: /grimoire add [category:] content")
            return
        rest = " ".join(args[1:])
        if ":" in args[1]:
            category, content = rest.split(":", 1)
            category = category.strip()
            content = content.strip()
        else:
            category = "general"
            content = rest.strip()
        if not content:
            self._set_status("Grimoire entry content cannot be empty.")
            return
        entry = self.grimoire.add(content, category)
        self._set_status(f"Grimoire entry added: [{entry.category}] 
{entry.entry_id}")

    elif sub == "list":
        active = self.grimoire.get_active()
        if not active:
            self._set_status("Grimoire is empty.")
            return
        lines = [f"[{e.entry_id}] [{e.category}] {e.content}" for e in active]
        self._set_status("Grimoire:\n" + "\n".join(lines))

    elif sub == "remove":
        if len(args) < 2:
            self._set_status("Usage: /grimoire remove <entry_id>")
            return
        ok = self.grimoire.remove(args[1])
        self._set_status(f"Removed {args[1]}." if ok else f"Entry not found: 
{args[1]}")

    elif sub == "restore":
        if len(args) < 2:
            self._set_status("Usage: /grimoire restore <entry_id>")
            return
        ok = self.grimoire.restore(args[1])
        self._set_status(f"Restored {args[1]}." if ok else f"Entry not found: 
{args[1]}")

    elif sub == "edit":
        if len(args) < 3:
            self._set_status("Usage: /grimoire edit <entry_id> <new content>")
            return
        new_content = " ".join(args[2:]).strip()
        ok = self.grimoire.edit(args[1], new_content)
        self._set_status(f"Updated {args[1]}." if ok else f"Entry not found: 
{args[1]}")

    elif sub == "status":
        usage = self.grimoire.token_usage()
        self._set_status(
            f"Grimoire: {usage['entry_count']} entries · "
            f"{usage['used']}/{usage['budget']} tokens ({usage['pct']}%)"
        )

    else:
        self._set_status(
            "Grimoire commands: add [cat:] content · list · remove <id> · "
            "restore <id> · edit <id> <content> · status"
        )
```

Then wire it into `_handle_menu_command` dispatch table:

```python
if cmd == "/grimoire":
    await self._handle_grimoire_command(argv)
    return
```

Stick with `if`.

class GrimoirePage(Screen):
"""
Grimoire management TUI.
Shows all entries (active + inactive) in a DataTable.
Provides add, remove, restore, edit controls.
Token budget bar shows current Grimoire fill.
"""

BINDINGS = [
("a", "add_entry", "Add"),
("d", "remove_entry", "Remove"),
("r", "restore_entry", "Restore"),
("e", "edit_entry", "Edit"),
("escape", "app.pop_screen", "Back"),
]

def compose(self) -> ComposeResult:
"""
Layout:
Top: Title label + token budget bar
Middle: DataTable (entry_id | active | category | content | source | created)
Bottom: Action buttons (Add / Remove / Restore / Edit) + Back
"""

yield Label(
f"◆ GRIMOIRE — {usage['used']}/{usage['budget']} tokens ({usage['pct']}%)",
id="grimoire-title"
)

with Horizontal(id="grimoire-actions"):
yield Button("Add [a]", id="btn-add", variant="primary")
yield Button("Remove [d]", id="btn-remove", variant="warning")
yield Button("Restore [r]", id="btn-restore", variant="default")
yield Button("Edit [e]", id="btn-edit", variant="default")
yield Button("Back [esc]", id="btn-back", variant="default")

def on_mount(self) -> None:
"""Populate DataTable with all Grimoire entries on page load."""

table.add_columns("ID", "●", "Category", "Content", "Source", "Created")
for entry in self.app.grimoire.get_all():

entry.entry_id,
"✓" if entry.active else "✗",
entry.category,
entry.content[:60] + "..." if len(entry.content) > 60 else entry.content,
entry.source,
entry.created_at[:10]
)

def action_add_entry(self) -> None:
"""Push AddEntryModal screen for content and category input."""
...

def action_remove_entry(self) -> None:
"""Soft-delete selected row entry. Refresh table. Update token bar."""
...

def action_restore_entry(self) -> None:
"""Restore selected inactive entry. Refresh table. Update token bar."""
...

def action_edit_entry(self) -> None:
"""Push EditEntryModal with current content pre-filled."""
...

def _build_token_bar(self, pct: int) -> Label:
"""
Render token budget as visual bar.
Same 8-segment pattern as StatusLayer context bar.
Color: mist below 75%, amber 75-89%, copper 90%+.
"""
filled = round(pct / 100 * 16) # 16 segments for wider display
bar = "▓" * filled + "░" * (16 - filled)
return Label(bar, id="grimoire-token-bar")

Claude:  Here's the implementation for the action methods:

```python
def action_add_entry(self) -> None:
    self.app.push_screen(AddEntryModal(), self._on_entry_added)

def _on_entry_added(self, result: tuple[str, str] | None) -> None:
    if not result:
        return
    content, category = result
    self.app.grimoire.add(content, category)
    self._refresh_table()

def action_remove_entry(self) -> None:
    table = self.query_one("#grimoire-table", DataTable)
    if table.cursor_row is None:
        return
    row = table.get_row_at(table.cursor_row)
    entry_id = str(row[0])
    self.app.grimoire.remove(entry_id)
    self._refresh_table()

def action_restore_entry(self) -> None:
    table = self.query_one("#grimoire-table", DataTable)
    if table.cursor_row is None:
        return
    row = table.get_row_at(table.cursor_row)
    entry_id = str(row[0])
    self.app.grimoire.restore(entry_id)
    self._refresh_table()

def action_edit_entry(self) -> None:
    table = self.query_one("#grimoire-table", DataTable)
    if table.cursor_row is None:
        return
    row = table.get_row_at(table.cursor_row)
    entry_id = str(row[0])
    entry = next((e for e in self.app.grimoire.get_all() if e.entry_id == 
entry_id), None)
    if not entry:
        return
    self.app.push_screen(EditEntryModal(entry_id, entry.content), 
self._on_entry_edited)

def _on_entry_edited(self, result: tuple[str, str] | None) -> None:
    if not result:
        return
    entry_id, new_content = result
    self.app.grimoire.edit(entry_id, new_content)
    self._refresh_table()

def on_button_pressed(self, event: Button.Pressed) -> None:
    if event.button.id == "btn-add":
        self.action_add_entry()
    elif event.button.id == "btn-remove":
        self.action_remove_entry()
    elif event.button.id == "btn-restore":
        self.action_restore_entry()
    elif event.button.id == "btn-edit":
        self.action_edit_entry()
    elif event.button.id == "btn-back":
        self.app.pop_screen()

def _refresh_table(self) -> None:
    table = self.query_one("#grimoire-table", DataTable)
    table.clear()
    for entry in self.app.grimoire.get_all():
        table.add_row(
            entry.entry_id,
            "✓" if entry.active else "✗",
            entry.category,
            entry.content[:60] + "..." if len(entry.content) > 60 else 
entry.content,
            entry.source,
            entry.created_at[:10]
        )
    usage = self.app.grimoire.token_usage()
    self.query_one("#grimoire-title", Label).update(
        f"◆ GRIMOIRE — {usage['used']}/{usage['budget']} tokens 
({usage['pct']}%)"
    )
    filled = round(usage['pct'] / 100 * 16)
    bar = "▓" * filled + "░" * (16 - filled)
    self.query_one("#grimoire-token-bar", Label).update(bar)
```

class AddEntryModal(Screen):
    def compose(self) -> ComposeResult:
        yield Label("◆ ADD GRIMOIRE ENTRY", id="modal-title")
        yield Input(placeholder="Category (e.g. communication_style)", 
id="modal-category")
        yield Input(placeholder="Content", id="modal-content")
        with Horizontal():
            yield Button("Add", id="modal-confirm", variant="primary")
            yield Button("Cancel", id="modal-cancel")

    def on_button_pressed(self, event: Button.Pressed) -> None:
        if event.button.id == "modal-confirm":
            content = self.query_one("#modal-content", Input).value.strip()
            category = self.query_one("#modal-category", Input).value.strip() 
or "general"
            if content:
                self.dismiss((content, category))
            else:
                self.dismiss(None)
        else:
            self.dismiss(None)


class EditEntryModal(Screen):
    def __init__(self, entry_id: str, current_content: str) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.entry_id = entry_id
        self.current_content = current_content

    def compose(self) -> ComposeResult:
        yield Label(f"◆ EDIT ENTRY — {self.entry_id}", id="modal-title")
        yield Input(value=self.current_content, id="modal-content")
        with Horizontal():
            yield Button("Save", id="modal-confirm", variant="primary")
            yield Button("Cancel", id="modal-cancel")

    def on_button_pressed(self, event: Button.Pressed) -> None:
        if event.button.id == "modal-confirm":
            new_content = self.query_one("#modal-content", Input).value.strip()
            if new_content:
                self.dismiss((self.entry_id, new_content))
            else:
                self.dismiss(None)
        else:
            self.dismiss(None)
```

LordFingers: def parse(self, raw: str) -> ParsedInput:
        s = (raw or "").replace("\x00", "").strip()
        if not s:
            return ParsedInput(kind="text", text="")
        if s.startswith("/"):
            parts = s.split(" ", 1)
            cmd = parts[0].strip()
            args = parts[1].strip() if len(parts) > 1 else ""
            return ParsedInput(kind="command", text=s, command=cmd, args=args)
        return ParsedInput(kind="text", text=s)

LordFingers: def _parse_argv(self, args: str) -> List[str]:        try:

Claude:  Two small bugs in `_estimate_tokens`:

**Bug 1:** `max(1, ...)` forces a minimum of 1 even on empty strings — that's 
why `token_usage()` returns 1 on empty Grimoire. Fix:

```python
def _estimate_tokens(self, text: str) -> int:
    if not text:
        return 0
    return max(1, int(len(text.split()) * 1.3))
```

**Bug 2:** The token budget test is failing by 1 — the estimate is slightly 
off because the header itself costs tokens and the budget math is tight. The 
`break` in `build_injection_string` happens after the cost check, meaning the 
header tokens aren't being subtracted accurately enough. The fix is already in 
the logic but the `max(1,...)` on the header string is inflating it. The empty 
string fix above will resolve this too since the header is non-empty and will 
estimate correctly.

Make that one change and run again.

LordFingers: # New method:
async def _handle_tome_command(self, args: list[str]) -> None:
"""
Dispatch /tome subcommands.
Guard: if not self.tome.is_active, show error bubble and return.
Otherwise mirrors _handle_grimoire_command() dispatch pattern.

Dispatch table:
[] → push TomePage screen
["add", ...] → parse category/content, call tome.add()
["list"] → render active entries as system bubble
["remove", id] → tome.remove(id), confirm
["restore", id]→ tome.restore(id), confirm
["edit", id, ...]→ tome.edit(id, content), confirm
["status"] → render token_usage() as system bubble
"""

await self._show_system_bubble(
"No project is active. Open a project to use the Tome."
)
return
...

Claude:  Add this method right below `_handle_grimoire_command` in `app.py`. Here's the 
full implementation:

```python
async def _handle_tome_command(self, args: list[str]) -> None:
    if not self.tome.is_active:
        self._set_status("No project is active. Open a project to use the 
Tome.")
        return

    if not args:
        self._set_status("Tome page not yet implemented. Use /tome list.")
        return

    sub = args[0].lower()

    if sub == "add":
        if len(args) < 2:
            self._set_status("Usage: /tome add [category:] content")
            return
        rest = " ".join(args[1:])
        if ":" in args[1]:
            category, content = rest.split(":", 1)
            category = category.strip()
            content = content.strip()
        else:
            category = "general"
            content = rest.strip()
        if not content:
            self._set_status("Tome entry content cannot be empty.")
            return
        entry = self.tome.add(content, category)
        if entry:
            self._set_status(f"Tome entry added: [{entry.category}] 
{entry.entry_id}")
        else:
            self._set_status("Failed to add Tome entry.")

    elif sub == "list":
        active = self.tome.get_active()
        if not active:
            self._set_status("Tome is empty.")
            return
        lines = [f"[{e.entry_id}] [{e.category}] {e.content}" for e in active]
        self._set_status("Tome:\n" + "\n".join(lines))

    elif sub == "remove":
        if len(args) < 2:
            self._set_status("Usage: /tome remove <entry_id>")
            return
        ok = self.tome.remove(args[1])
        self._set_status(f"Removed {args[1]}." if ok else f"Entry not found: 
{args[1]}")

    elif sub == "restore":
        if len(args) < 2:
            self._set_status("Usage: /tome restore <entry_id>")
            return
        ok = self.tome.restore(args[1])
        self._set_status(f"Restored {args[1]}." if ok else f"Entry not found: 
{args[1]}")

    elif sub == "edit":
        if len(args) < 3:
            self._set_status("Usage: /tome edit <entry_id> <new content>")
            return
        new_content = " ".join(args[2:]).strip()
        ok = self.tome.edit(args[1], new_content)
        self._set_status(f"Updated {args[1]}." if ok else f"Entry not found: 
{args[1]}")

    elif sub == "status":
        usage = self.tome.token_usage()
        self._set_status(
            f"Tome: {usage['entry_count']} entries · "
            f"{usage['used']}/{usage['budget']} tokens ({usage['pct']}%)"
        )

    else:
        self._set_status(
            "Tome commands: add [cat:] content · list · remove <id> · "
            "restore <id> · edit <id> <content> · status"
        )
```

Then wire it in `_handle_menu_command` right after the grimoire dispatch:

```python
if cmd == "/tome":
    await self._handle_tome_command(argv)
    return
```

# Phase 5: distillation trigger
if self.distillation.should_distill(thread.messages, getattr(self.cfg.memory, 
'distillation_threshold', 6000)):
    result = self.distillation.distill(
        thread.messages,
        extract_to_chronicle=True,
        chronicle=self.vectors,
        reflection=self.reflection
    )
    messages.append(result.compressed_message)
    self.animation_controller.fire_event('distillation')
    self.status_layer.update_status(
        distillation_count=self.status_layer.state.distillation_count + 1
    )
    return messages  # Skip short_max loop — context is now the compressed 
message
```

Add the actual implementation after the closing `"""`:

```python
def extract_routing_signals(self, messages: list[dict]) -> dict:
        """..."""
        import re, json
        from datetime import datetime, timezone
        from collections import Counter

        user_messages = [m for m in messages if m.get("role") == "user"]
        all_content = " ".join(m.get("content", "") for m in user_messages)

        stopwords = {"the","a","an","is","it","i","to","of","in","for",
                     "that","this","and","or","but","with","be","was","are"}
        words = [w.lower() for w in re.findall(r"\b\w{4,}\b", all_content)
                 if w.lower() not in stopwords]
        top_topics = [w for w,_ in Counter(words).most_common(3)]

        signals = {
            "timestamp": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
            "dominant_topics": top_topics,
            "message_length_avg": (
                sum(len(m.get("content", "").split()) for m in user_messages)
                / max(len(user_messages), 1)
            ),
            "code_present": bool(re.search(r"```|def |class |import ", 
all_content)),
            "question_count": sum(
                1 for m in user_messages if m.get("content", 
"").rstrip().endswith("?")
            ),
            "turn_count": len(messages),
        }

        from pathlib import Path
        log_path = Path(self.cfg.storage.reflection_log_path)
        log_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        with log_path.open("a") as f:
            f.write(json.dumps(signals) + "\n")

        return signals
```

```

def test_chronicle_instantiates(tmp_path):
    """Chronicle instantiates and creates store file correctly."""
    from unittest.mock import MagicMock
    from memory.chronicle import Chronicle
    mock_cfg = MagicMock()
    mock_cfg.storage.vectors_path = str(tmp_path / "chronicle.pkl")
    mock_cfg.models.embeddings = "text-embedding-3-small"
    mock_cfg.memory.min_relevance_score = 0.0
    c = Chronicle(cfg=mock_cfg, client=MagicMock())
    assert c is not None

def test_chronicle_add_and_retrieve(tmp_path):
    """Chronicle add() and query() work correctly under new name."""
    from unittest.mock import MagicMock

def test_distill_extracts_to_chronicle(tmp_path):
    """distill() with extract_to_chronicle=True calls 
chronicle.add_from_distillation()."""
    from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch
    from memory.distillation import Distillation
    d = Distillation(api_client=None)
    mock_chronicle = MagicMock()
    mock_reflection = MagicMock()
    mock_reflection.extract_routing_signals.return_value = {}
    messages = [
        {"role": "user", "content": "We decided to use async workers."},
        {"role": "assistant", "content": "Got it."},
        {"role": "user", "content": "The constraint is no blocking calls."},
        {"role": "assistant", "content": "Understood."},
    ]
    result = d.distill(messages, extract_to_chronicle=True,
                       chronicle=mock_chronicle, reflection=mock_reflection)
    assert mock_chronicle.add_from_distillation.called

def test_chronicle_add_from_distillation(tmp_path):
    """add_from_distillation() adds entry with source=distillation metadata."""
    from unittest.mock import MagicMock
    from memory.chronicle import Chronicle
    mock_cfg = MagicMock()
    mock_cfg.storage.vectors_path = str(tmp_path / "chronicle.pkl")
    mock_cfg.models.embeddings = "text-embedding-3-small"
    mock_cfg.memory.min_relevance_score = 0.0
    mock_client = MagicMock()
    mock_client.embeddings.create.return_value.data[0].embedding = [0.1] * 1536
    c = Chronicle(cfg=mock_cfg, client=mock_client)
    c.add_from_distillation("We decided to use async workers.")
    assert len(c.entries) >= 1
    assert c.entries[-1].metadata.get("source") == "distillation"

def test_extract_routing_signals_returns_dict(tmp_path):
    """extract_routing_signals() returns dict with expected keys."""
    from unittest.mock import MagicMock
    from client.reflection import Reflection
    log = tmp_path / "reflections.jsonl"
    mock_cfg = MagicMock()
    mock_cfg.storage.reflection_log_path = str(log)
    r = Reflection(mock_cfg, client=MagicMock(), chronicle=MagicMock())
    messages = [{"role": "user", "content": "What is the best approach?"}]
    result = r.extract_routing_signals(messages)
    assert isinstance(result, dict)
    for key in ("dominant_topics", "message_length_avg", "code_present",
                "question_count", "turn_count"):
        assert key in result

    from unittest.mock import MagicMock
    from client.reflection import Reflection

    r.extract_routing_signals(messages)

LordFingers: k, decision logs, deviations from spec, friction points

Claude:  **Decision Log — Phase 5**

- `Distillation.__init__` signature changed from `(cfg, client=...)` to 
`(api_client=None)` — cfg not needed, api_client is the only dependency
- `self.vectors` renamed to `self.chronicle` throughout `app.py` and 
`client/reflection.py` — Phase 1 naming pass item missed at the time
- `vectors=` kwarg in `Reflection` instantiation corrected to `chronicle=` to 
match rename
- Distillation trigger added to `_build_context()` before the `short_max` loop 
— fires synchronously before API call, returns early with compressed message 
if triggered
- `Chronicle.add_from_distillation()` appended to `memory/chronicle.py` — 
thin wrapper over `add()` stamping `source=distillation`
- `Reflection.extract_routing_signals()` appended to `client/reflection.py` — 
writes signal record to `reflections.jsonl`, returns dict for immediate use
- `sigils/` directory and `default.txt` created — was missing, caused two 
Phase 2 tests to fail
- FAT_PATTERNS had a split string across two list entries — merged into 
single valid regex
- `memory/tome.py` was never written to disk despite being designed — created 
during Phase 5 test run when Phase 4 tests first ran
- App restored from `phase-4-tome` commit after `app.py` was accidentally 
overwritten by an older version from an open text editor

---

**Deviations from Spec**

| Section | What Spec Said | What I Did Instead | Why / Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Distillation.__init__` | `(cfg, client=...)` | `(api_client=None)` | cfg not 
needed in Phase 5 design. Permanent. |
| `self.vectors` | Should have been `self.chronicle` in Phase 1 | Renamed in 
Phase 5 | Missed in Phase 1. Corrected. Permanent. |
| `Reflection` kwarg | `vectors=` | `chronicle=` | Follows rename. Permanent. |
| `memory/tome.py` | Created in Phase 4 | Actually created in Phase 5 | File 
was designed but never written to disk. Permanent. |

---

**Friction Points**
- `app.py` accidentally overwritten by open text editor — restored from 
`phase-4-tome` git commit
- Phase 4 and 5 changes had to be re-applied after restore — `self.vectors` 
rename, Distillation signature fix, distillation trigger, Grimoire store path
- FAT_PATTERNS broken regex — pattern split across two string literals in the 
list
- `memory/tome.py` missing from disk despite being fully designed — silent 
until Phase 4 tests ran for the first time in Phase 5
- `sigils/` directory never created — Phase 2 tests failing silently until 
Phase 5 run
- Chronicle tests failing with `EOFError` — mocked cfg pointing to real 
(empty) vectors file, needed `tmp_path` override
- Reflection tests using `log_path=` kwarg that doesn't exist — tests written 
against imagined API, not real signature
- `_extract_chronicle_entries` word count threshold (15) meant short test 
messages never extracted — required longer fixture messages to pass

LordFingers: 8. The Assessor Entity — Full Definition
The Assessor is the most behaviorally distinct Entity in Phase 6. It does not 
assist. It does not answer questions. When summoned, it reads the current 
Thread and the Chronicle, produces a structured profile observation of the 
Wizard, and writes its findings to the Grimoire with source='assessor'.

8.1 Assessor Behavioral Contract
Rule
Description
Summoned only
The Assessor never appears uninvited. summoned_only: true in assessor.yaml. 
uninvited_eligible: false.
One response per summon
The Assessor produces its observation and then the Council returns to 
Luminarious automatically. It does not hold the active position.
Third person, clinical
The Assessor refers to the Wizard as 'the Wizard' or 'LordFingers'. Never 
'you'. No second-person address.
Evidence only
Speculation trait 0.05. Every observation must cite the specific message or 
pattern it is derived from.
Writes to Grimoire
After producing its response, the Assessor calls grimoire.add() with 
source='assessor' for each substantive observation.
Does not write if nothing new
If the current Thread offers no new observations not already in the Grimoire, 
the Assessor says so explicitly and writes nothing.
Maximum precision
precision trait 0.98. Exact language. No hedging language in observation 
statements.

8.2 Assessor Observation Format
The Assessor structures its response as a set of categorized observations. Each 
observation is a single precise statement with an evidence marker.
THE ASSESSOR — Profile Observation
Thread: {N} turns | Session: {timestamp}

COMMUNICATION_STYLE:
- Prefers direct questions over exploratory framing. [T7, T12, T18]
- Uses code blocks to communicate structural intent, not just syntax. [T3, T15]

WORK_PATTERNS:
- Operates in sequential phase-based development. Resists parallelization. 
[T4-T9]
- Revisits architectural decisions under implementation pressure. [T11, T22]

FRICTION_POINTS:
- Thread compression (distillation) triggers user-visible anxiety about context 
loss. [T14]

PREFERENCES:
- Favors lore-named vocabulary over technical terminology in project 
documentation. [T2, T6]

NEW_GRIMOIRE_ENTRIES: 3
SKIPPED (already in Grimoire): 1


# In app.py _handle_summon_command() for assessor:

async def _run_assessor(self) -> None:
"""
Assessor summon procedure:
1. Compile and set Assessor as active Entity
2. Build Assessor context:
- Assessor instruction_str as system message
- Grimoire injection (read only — Assessor reads current Grimoire)
- Chronicle top-k retrieval for "wizard profile patterns preferences"
- Current Thread history

profile observation. Write new findings to the Grimoire."
3. API call with Assessor sampling profile (precise)
4. Render response as LuminariousBubble in crimson (#8B2020)
5. Parse response for new Grimoire entries:
- Extract observations from COMMUNICATION_STYLE, WORK_PATTERNS,
FRICTION_POINTS, PREFERENCES sections
- For each observation not already in Grimoire (fuzzy match):
grimoire.add(content=observation, category=section, source="assessor")
6. Dismiss Assessor — return to Luminarious
7. Update StatusLayer: entity back to LUMINARIOUS / C9A84C

async def _handle_summon_command(self, args: list[str]) -> None:
    if not args:
        # List available entities
        from entities.entity_compiler import EntityCompilationError
        canon = self.council.get_canon()
        lines = [f"[{e['entity_id']}] {e['display_name']} #{e['color_hex']}" 
for e in canon]

        return

    entity_id = args[0].lower().strip()

    if entity_id == "luminarious":

        self.council.summon(entity_id)
        entity = self.council.get_active()
        self.status_layer.update_status(
            entity_name=entity.display_name,
            entity_color=entity.color_hex
        )

Claude:  ```python
async def _handle_tome_command(self, args: list[str]) -> None:
    if not self.tome.is_active:
        self._set_status("No project is active. Open a project to use the 
Tome.")
        return

    if not args:
        self._set_status("Tome page not yet implemented. Use /tome list.")
        return

    sub = args[0].lower()

    if sub == "add":
        if len(args) < 2:
            self._set_status("Usage: /tome add [category:] content")
            return
        rest = " ".join(args[1:])
        if ":" in args[1]:
            category, content = rest.split(":", 1)
            category = category.strip()
            content = content.strip()
        else:
            category = "general"
            content = rest.strip()
        if not content:
            self._set_status("Tome entry content cannot be empty.")
            return
        entry = self.tome.add(content, category)
        if entry:
            self._set_status(f"Tome entry added: [{entry.category}] 
{entry.entry_id}")
        else:
            self._set_status("Failed to add Tome entry.")

    elif sub == "list":
        active = self.tome.get_active()
        if not active:
            self._set_status("Tome is empty.")
            return
        lines = [f"[{e.entry_id}] [{e.category}] {e.content}" for e in active]
        self._set_status("Tome:\n" + "\n".join(lines))

    elif sub == "remove":
        if len(args) < 2:
            self._set_status("Usage: /tome remove <entry_id>")
            return
        ok = self.tome.remove(args[1])
        self._set_status(f"Removed {args[1]}." if ok else f"Entry not found: 
{args[1]}")

    elif sub == "restore":
        if len(args) < 2:
            self._set_status("Usage: /tome restore <entry_id>")
            return
        ok = self.tome.restore(args[1])
        self._set_status(f"Restored {args[1]}." if ok else f"Entry not found: 
{args[1]}")

    elif sub == "edit":
        if len(args) < 3:
            self._set_status("Usage: /tome edit <entry_id> <new content>")
            return
        new_content = " ".join(args[2:]).strip()
        ok = self.tome.edit(args[1], new_content)
        self._set_status(f"Updated {args[1]}." if ok else f"Entry not found: 
{args[1]}")

    elif sub == "status":
        usage = self.tome.token_usage()
        self._set_status(
            f"Tome: {usage['entry_count']} entries · "
            f"{usage['used']}/{usage['budget']} tokens ({usage['pct']}%)"
        )

    else:
        self._set_status(
            "Tome commands: add [cat:] content · list · remove <id> · "
            "restore <id> · edit <id> <content> · status"
        )
```

```python
async def _handle_summon_command(self, args: list[str]) -> None:
    if not args:
        try:
            import yaml
            with open("entities/canon/entity_canon.yaml") as f:
                canon = yaml.safe_load(f)
            lines = [
                f"[{e['entity_id']}] {e['display_name']} #{e['color_hex']}"
                for e in canon.get("entities", [])
            ]
            self._set_status("Entities:\n" + "\n".join(lines))
        except Exception as e:
            self._set_status(f"Could not load entity canon: {e}")
        return

    entity_id = args[0].lower().strip()

    if entity_id == "luminarious":
        self._set_status("Luminarious is already the anchor. Nothing to 
summon.")
        return

    if entity_id == "assessor":
        await self._run_assessor()
        return

    try:
        self.council.summon(entity_id)
        entity = self.council.get_active()
        self.status_layer.update_status(
            entity_name=entity.display_name,
            entity_color=entity.color_hex
        )
        self._set_status(f"Summoned: {entity.display_name}")
    except Exception as e:
        self._set_status(f"Summon failed: {e}")
```

Then `_run_assessor`:

```python
async def _run_assessor(self) -> None:
    if not self.conversations.active:
        self._set_status("No conversation loaded. Assessor requires an active 
Thread.")
        return

    # 1. Compile and set Assessor as active
    try:
        self.council.summon("assessor")
    except Exception as e:
        self._set_status(f"Assessor compilation failed: {e}")
        return

    assessor = self.council.get_active()
    self.status_layer.update_status(
        entity_name=assessor.display_name,
        entity_color=assessor.color_hex
    )

    # 2. Build Assessor context
    thread = self.conversations.get_thread(
        self.conversations.active.active_thread_id
    )
    messages = []

    # Assessor instruction string
    messages.append({
        "role": "system",
        "content": assessor.instruction_str
    })

    # Grimoire injection — read only
    grimoire_injection = self.grimoire.build_injection_string()
    if grimoire_injection:
        messages.append({"role": "system", "content": grimoire_injection})

    # Chronicle retrieval — wizard profile patterns
    retrieved = self.chronicle.query(
        "wizard profile patterns preferences communication style",
        top_k=5,
        conversation_ids=[self.conversations.active.id],
        thread_by_conversation={
            self.conversations.active.id: 
self.conversations.active.active_thread_id
        }
    )
    if retrieved:
        blob = "\n\n".join(f"[score={r['score']:.3f}] {r['text']}" for r in 
retrieved)
        messages.append({"role": "system", "content": "Chronicle fragments:\n" 
+ blob})

    # Current thread history
    for m in thread.messages[-20:]:
        messages.append({"role": m.get("role", ""), "content": m.get("content", 
"")})

    # Assessor task prompt
    messages.append({
        "role": "user",
        "content": (
            "Analyze this Thread. Produce a structured profile observation "
            "of the Wizard. Use the format specified in your instructions. "
            "Write new findings to the Grimoire. Skip observations already 
present."
        )
    })

    # 3. API call with Assessor sampling profile
    try:
        profile = assessor.sampling_profile
        response_text = await asyncio.to_thread(
            self._assessor_api_call,
            messages,
            profile
        )
    except Exception as e:
        self._set_status(f"Assessor API call failed: {e}")
        self.council.dismiss()
        return

    # 4. Render response
    self._mount_combo(
        self.middle.current_turn,
        header="THE ASSESSOR",
        body=response_text,
        render_mode="markdown",
        align="full",
        combo_classes="system assessor",
    )

    # 5. Parse and write Grimoire entries
    written, skipped = self._assessor_write_grimoire(response_text)

    # 6. Dismiss Assessor
    self.council.dismiss()
    entity = self.council.get_active()

    # 7. Update StatusLayer
    self.status_layer.update_status(
        entity_name=entity.display_name,
        entity_color=entity.color_hex
    )
    self._set_status(
        f"Assessor complete. {written} observations written · {skipped} 
skipped. "
        f"Returned to {entity.display_name}."
    )


def _assessor_api_call(self, messages: list, profile: dict) -> str:
    gen, _meta = self.router.stream_response_text(
        self.router.decide("assessor profile observation").model,
        messages,
        temperature=profile.get("temperature", 0.2),
        max_output_tokens=profile.get("max_output_tokens", 1500),
    )
    return "".join(gen)


def _assessor_write_grimoire(self, response_text: str) -> tuple[int, int]:
    import re
    sections = {
        "COMMUNICATION_STYLE": "communication_style",
        "WORK_PATTERNS": "work_patterns",
        "FRICTION_POINTS": "friction_points",
        "PREFERENCES": "preferences",
    }
    written = 0
    skipped = 0
    active_entries = [e.content.lower() for e in self.grimoire.get_active()]

    for section_header, category in sections.items():
        pattern = rf"{section_header}:\s*\n((?:\s*-[^\n]+\n?)+)"
        match = re.search(pattern, response_text)
        if not match:
            continue
        block = match.group(1)
        observations = re.findall(r"-\s*(.+?)(?:\s*\[.*?\])?$", block, 
re.MULTILINE)
        for obs in observations:
            obs = obs.strip()
            if not obs:
                continue
            obs_lower = obs.lower()
            # Fuzzy match — skip if very similar to existing entry
            if any(
                len(set(obs_lower.split()) & set(e.split())) / 
max(len(obs_lower.split()), 1) > 0.6
                for e in active_entries
            ):
                skipped += 1
                continue
            self.grimoire.add(obs, category, source="assessor")
            written += 1

    return written, skipped
```

LordFingers: 2031 ui/app.py

Claude:  ```bash
python3 - <<'EOF'
with open('ui/app.py', 'r') as f:
    lines = f.readlines()

new_methods = """
    async def _handle_tome_command(self, args: list[str]) -> None:
        if not self.tome.is_active:
            self._set_status("No project is active. Open a project to use the 
Tome.")
            return
        if not args:
            self._set_status("Tome page not yet implemented. Use /tome list.")
            return
        sub = args[0].lower()
        if sub == "add":
            if len(args) < 2:
                self._set_status("Usage: /tome add [category:] content")
                return
            rest = " ".join(args[1:])
            if ":" in args[1]:
                category, content = rest.split(":", 1)
                category = category.strip()
                content = content.strip()
            else:
                category = "general"
                content = rest.strip()
            if not content:
                self._set_status("Tome entry content cannot be empty.")
                return
            entry = self.tome.add(content, category)
            if entry:
                self._set_status(f"Tome entry added: [{entry.category}] 
{entry.entry_id}")
            else:
                self._set_status("Failed to add Tome entry.")
        elif sub == "list":
            active = self.tome.get_active()
            if not active:
                self._set_status("Tome is empty.")
                return
            lines = [f"[{e.entry_id}] [{e.category}] {e.content}" for e in 
active]
            self._set_status("Tome:\\n" + "\\n".join(lines))
        elif sub == "remove":
            if len(args) < 2:
                self._set_status("Usage: /tome remove <entry_id>")
                return
            ok = self.tome.remove(args[1])
            self._set_status(f"Removed {args[1]}." if ok else f"Entry not 
found: {args[1]}")
        elif sub == "restore":
            if len(args) < 2:
                self._set_status("Usage: /tome restore <entry_id>")
                return
            ok = self.tome.restore(args[1])
            self._set_status(f"Restored {args[1]}." if ok else f"Entry not 
found: {args[1]}")
        elif sub == "edit":
            if len(args) < 3:
                self._set_status("Usage: /tome edit <entry_id> <new content>")
                return
            new_content = " ".join(args[2:]).strip()
            ok = self.tome.edit(args[1], new_content)
            self._set_status(f"Updated {args[1]}." if ok else f"Entry not 
found: {args[1]}")
        elif sub == "status":
            usage = self.tome.token_usage()
            self._set_status(
                f"Tome: {usage['entry_count']} entries · "
                f"{usage['used']}/{usage['budget']} tokens ({usage['pct']}%)"
            )
        else:
            self._set_status(
                "Tome commands: add [cat:] content · list · remove <id> · "
                "restore <id> · edit <id> <content> · status"
            )

    async def _handle_summon_command(self, args: list[str]) -> None:
        if not args:
            try:
                import yaml
                with open("entities/canon/entity_canon.yaml") as f:
                    canon = yaml.safe_load(f)
                lines = [
                    f"[{e['entity_id']}] {e['display_name']} #{e['color_hex']}"
                    for e in canon.get("entities", [])
                ]
                self._set_status("Entities:\\n" + "\\n".join(lines))
            except Exception as e:
                self._set_status(f"Could not load entity canon: {e}")
            return
        entity_id = args[0].lower().strip()
        if entity_id == "luminarious":
            self._set_status("Luminarious is already the anchor. Nothing to 
summon.")
            return
        if entity_id == "assessor":
            await self._run_assessor()
            return
        try:
            self.council.summon(entity_id)
            entity = self.council.get_active()
            self.status_layer.update_status(
                entity_name=entity.display_name,
                entity_color=entity.color_hex
            )
            self._set_status(f"Summoned: {entity.display_name}")
        except Exception as e:
            self._set_status(f"Summon failed: {e}")

    async def _run_assessor(self) -> None:
        if not self.conversations.active:
            self._set_status("No conversation loaded. Assessor requires an 
active Thread.")
            return
        try:
            self.council.summon("assessor")
        except Exception as e:
            self._set_status(f"Assessor compilation failed: {e}")
            return
        assessor = self.council.get_active()
        self.status_layer.update_status(
            entity_name=assessor.display_name,
            entity_color=assessor.color_hex
        )
        thread = self.conversations.get_thread(
            self.conversations.active.active_thread_id
        )
        messages = []
        messages.append({"role": "system", "content": assessor.instruction_str})
        grimoire_injection = self.grimoire.build_injection_string()
        if grimoire_injection:
            messages.append({"role": "system", "content": grimoire_injection})
        retrieved = self.chronicle.query(
            "wizard profile patterns preferences communication style",
            top_k=5,
            conversation_ids=[self.conversations.active.id],
            thread_by_conversation={
                self.conversations.active.id: 
self.conversations.active.active_thread_id
            }
        )
        if retrieved:
            blob = "\\n\\n".join(f"[score={r['score']:.3f}] {r['text']}" for r 
in retrieved)
            messages.append({"role": "system", "content": "Chronicle 
fragments:\\n" + blob})
        for m in thread.messages[-20:]:
            messages.append({"role": m.get("role", ""), "content": 
m.get("content", "")})
        messages.append({
            "role": "user",
            "content": (
                "Analyze this Thread. Produce a structured profile observation "
                "of the Wizard. Use the format specified in your instructions. "
                "Write new findings to the Grimoire. Skip observations already 
present."
            )
        })
        try:
            profile = assessor.sampling_profile
            response_text = await asyncio.to_thread(
                self._assessor_api_call,
                messages,
                profile
            )
        except Exception as e:
            self._set_status(f"Assessor API call failed: {e}")
            self.council.dismiss()
            return
        self._mount_combo(
            self.middle.current_turn,
            header="THE ASSESSOR",
            body=response_text,
            render_mode="markdown",
            align="full",
            combo_classes="system assessor",
        )
        written, skipped = self._assessor_write_grimoire(response_text)
        self.council.dismiss()
        entity = self.council.get_active()
        self.status_layer.update_status(
            entity_name=entity.display_name,
            entity_color=entity.color_hex
        )
        self._set_status(
            f"Assessor complete. {written} observations written · {skipped} 
skipped. "
            f"Returned to {entity.display_name}."
        )

    def _assessor_api_call(self, messages: list, profile: dict) -> str:
        gen, _meta = self.router.stream_response_text(
            self.router.decide("assessor profile observation").model,
            messages,
            temperature=profile.get("temperature", 0.2),
            max_output_tokens=profile.get("max_output_tokens", 1500),
        )
        return "".join(gen)

    def _assessor_write_grimoire(self, response_text: str) -> tuple[int, int]:
        import re
        sections = {
            "COMMUNICATION_STYLE": "communication_style",
            "WORK_PATTERNS": "work_patterns",
            "FRICTION_POINTS": "friction_points",
            "PREFERENCES": "preferences",
        }
        written = 0
        skipped = 0
        active_entries = [e.content.lower() for e in self.grimoire.get_active()]
        for section_header, category in sections.items():
            pattern = rf"{section_header}:\\s*\\n((?:\\s*-[^\\n]+\\n?)+)"
            match = re.search(pattern, response_text)
            if not match:
                continue
            block = match.group(1)
            observations = re.findall(r"-\\s*(.+?)(?:\\s*\\[.*?\\])?$", block, 
re.MULTILINE)
            for obs in observations:
                obs = obs.strip()
                if not obs:
                    continue
                obs_lower = obs.lower()
                if any(
                    len(set(obs_lower.split()) & set(e.split())) / 
max(len(obs_lower.split()), 1) > 0.6
                    for e in active_entries
                ):
                    skipped += 1
                    continue
                self.grimoire.add(obs, category, source="assessor")
                written += 1
        return written, skipped

"""

# Insert before line 1494 (0-indexed: 1493)
lines.insert(1493, new_methods)

with open('ui/app.py', 'w') as f:
    f.writelines(lines)
print("Done")
EOF
```

LordFingers: well wait. how much of "entities" behaviour relies on temperature? I am 
using  models:
  smart: "gpt-5.2"
  fast: "gpt-5-mini"
  nano: "gpt-5-nano"
  summary: "gpt-5-nano"
  embeddings: "text-embedding-3-small"
  code: "gpt-5-mini"

woul 4 series be better?

Claude:  Good question. `gpt-5` series via the Responses API doesn't support 
`temperature` as a parameter — it's a reasoning model family where 
temperature is fixed. The trait system's behavioral differentiation relies on 
the instruction string, not sampling parameters. Temperature was always a 
secondary lever.

For Entity behavioral distinction, the instruction string does the heavy 
lifting — verbosity, challenge, precision, speculation are all encoded in the 
prompt. Temperature would have been a fine-tuning nudge at best.

**Options:**

1. **Keep gpt-5 series** — drop `temperature` from API calls entirely, rely 
100% on instruction strings for Entity differentiation. This is actually 
cleaner and more aligned with how the spec describes compilation.

2. **Switch to gpt-4o** — supports temperature, gives you the sampling 
profile knobs as designed. Slower, costs more.

For a v1.0 proof of concept where the goal is to validate Entity behavioral 
differences through real use, option 1 is the right call. The instruction 
strings are where the real differentiation lives anyway.

Recommendation: strip `temperature` and `top_p` from all API calls, keep 
`max_output_tokens`. The Entity profiles still matter — they control response 
length limits. Note this as a permanent deviation in the decision log.

Want to proceed with that?

LordFingers: 4 costs more? ok we'll go with option 1

Claude:  ```bash

That won't work reliably on multiline. Do it properly:

content = content.replace(
    '            temperature=temperature,\n',
    ''
)

with open('client/router.py', 'w') as f:
    f.write(content)
print("Done")
EOF
```

Claude:  The router is working fine. The issue is the `messages` list being passed — 
specifically the `input` format. The Responses API may be rejecting messages 
with empty `role` or `content`. Check if any messages in the Assessor context 
have empty role:

The thread messages are being iterated with `m.get("role", "")` — if role is 
missing it passes an empty string, which the API rejects silently.

Fix in `_run_assessor`:

```bash
python3 - <<'EOF'
with open('ui/app.py', 'r') as f:
    content = f.read()

content = content.replace(
    '        for m in thread.messages[-20:]:\n            
messages.append({"role": m.get("role", ""), "content": m.get("content", "")})',
    '        for m in thread.messages[-20:]:\n            role = m.get("role", 
"")\n            content_text = m.get("content", "").strip()\n            if 
role in ("user", "assistant") and content_text:\n                
messages.append({"role": role, "content": content_text})'

Claude:  The Responses API is receiving system role messages in `input` and silently 
dropping them. Fix `stream_response_text` to accept an optional `instructions` 
parameter and filter system messages out of input:

```bash
python3 - <<'EOF'
with open('client/router.py', 'r') as f:
    content = f.read()

old = '''    def stream_response_text(
        self,
        model: str,
        input_messages: List[Dict],
        *,
        max_output_tokens: Optional[int] = None,
    ) -> Tuple[Generator[str, None, None], Dict]:
        meta: Dict = {"usage": None}
        stream = self.client.responses.create(
            model=model,
            input=input_messages,
            max_output_tokens=max_output_tokens,
            stream=True,
        )'''

new = '''    def stream_response_text(
        self,
        model: str,
        input_messages: List[Dict],
        *,
        max_output_tokens: Optional[int] = None,
        instructions: Optional[str] = None,
    ) -> Tuple[Generator[str, None, None], Dict]:
        meta: Dict = {"usage": None}
        # Responses API only accepts user/assistant roles in input
        filtered = [m for m in input_messages if m.get("role") in ("user", 
"assistant")]
        # Collect system messages as instructions if not provided
        if instructions is None:
            system_parts = [m["content"] for m in input_messages if 
m.get("role") == "system"]
            if system_parts:
                instructions = "\\n\\n".join(system_parts)
        create_kwargs = dict(
            model=model,
            input=filtered,
            max_output_tokens=max_output_tokens,
            stream=True,
        )
        if instructions:
            create_kwargs["instructions"] = instructions
        stream = self.client.responses.create(**create_kwargs)'''

with open('client/router.py', 'w') as f:
    f.write(content.replace(old, new))
print("Done")
EOF
```

with open('client/router.py', 'r') as f:
    lines = f.readlines()

# Find and remove the duplicate orphan lines
cleaned = []
skip_next = 0
for i, line in enumerate(lines):
    if skip_next > 0:
        skip_next -= 1
        continue
    # Detect the orphaned duplicate block
    if '            system_parts = [m["content"] for m in input_messages if 
m.get("role") == "system"]' in line:
        # Check if this is a duplicate (already have filtered above)
        # Look back — if we already passed the create_kwargs block, skip these
        prev = ''.join(lines[max(0,i-5):i])
        if 'create_kwargs["instructions"]' in prev:
            skip_next = 2  # skip this line and next 2
            continue
    cleaned.append(line)

with open('client/router.py', 'w') as f:
    f.writelines(cleaned)
print("Done")

LordFingers: sampling_profile: {'temperature': 0.2, 'top_p': 0.85, 'max_output_tokens': 1500}

Claude:  The profile is correct. The `response.incomplete` with no output text at all 
suggests the input is too large — the massive Luminarious instruction string 
from the messages log is being passed as `instructions`. 

The Assessor `messages` list contains system messages including the full 
Luminarious compiled instruction from `self.grimoire.build_injection_string()` 
or from the thread history. The `stream_response_text` collects ALL system 
messages into `instructions` — including the massive Luminarious one that was 
injected into the thread context.

The fix: `_run_assessor` should pass `instructions` explicitly rather than 
letting `stream_response_text` auto-collect system messages. Update 
`_assessor_api_call`:

```bash
python3 - <<'EOF'
with open('ui/app.py', 'r') as f:
    content = f.read()

old = '''    def _assessor_api_call(self, messages: list, profile: dict) -> str:
        gen, _meta = self.router.stream_response_text(
            self.router.decide("assessor profile observation").model,
            messages,
            max_output_tokens=profile.get("max_output_tokens", 1500),
        )
        return "".join(gen)'''

new = '''    def _assessor_api_call(self, messages: list, profile: dict) -> str:
        # Extract system messages as instructions, pass only user/assistant to 
input
        system_parts = [m["content"] for m in messages if m.get("role") == 
"system"]
        instructions = "\\n\\n".join(system_parts) if system_parts else None
        filtered = [m for m in messages if m.get("role") in ("user", 
"assistant")]
        gen, _meta = self.router.stream_response_text(
            self.router.decide("assessor profile observation").model,
            filtered,
            max_output_tokens=profile.get("max_output_tokens", 1500),
            instructions=instructions,
        )
        return "".join(gen)'''

with open('ui/app.py', 'w') as f:
    f.write(content.replace(old, new))
print("Done")
EOF
```

with open('ui/app.py', 'r') as f:
    content = f.read()

old = '''    def _assessor_api_call(self, messages: list, profile: dict) -> str:
        # Extract system messages as instructions, pass only user/assistant to
